No new IDENTITY/CHAR/UNIT/READY. Historical Seven Sisters remains distinct from WITCH NUMBER4 and777☆SISTERS. Prior revisions, correction44 and full readiness histories remain. Names follow the existing routes: Coney is the presented rokusaki-connie-presented identity; Player is the established player-protagonist, not a guessed real-world name. In Long Night's commission the requester addressed as manager is Coney, whereas the separately invoked absent shihainin is Player. The unnamed lottery attendant remains a document-local role. An unnamed written whistle is non-character source text. The fantasy companions and group speech labels remain their actual authored forms, not new CHAR or UNIT identities. Quoted Coney in the cafe opening is Player's report rather than an on-scene Coney voice.
